## Select Luxury Items Tax Act {#select-luxury-items-tax-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 2022, c. 10, s. 135
:::

Assented to 2022-06-23

An Act respecting the taxation of select luxury items

::: ReaderNote
\[Enacted by section 135 of chapter 10 of the Statutes of Canada, 2022,
in force or is deemed to have come into force on September 1, 2022;
provisions that set out the tax on subject aircraft in force September
1, 2022, see SI/2022-36.\]
:::

:::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1363087 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Select Luxury Items Tax Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35).

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Select Luxury Items Tax]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1363090 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1363091 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363092 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Act.

  [[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assessment under this
      Act and includes a reassessment. ([cotisation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 2 of the
      [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) or an [[authorized foreign
      bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in that section, that is
      not subject to the restrictions and requirements referred to in
      subsection 524(2) of that Act. ([banque]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[calendar quarter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[calendar quarter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a period of three
      months beginning on the first day of January, April, July or
      October. ([trimestre civil]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in sections 80
      and 81 and subsections 153(1) to (8) and (19), the Commissioner of
      Revenue appointed under section 25 of the [Canada Revenue Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.11). ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an individual at a
      particular time means a person who is the common-law partner of
      the individual at the particular time for the purposes of the
      [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([conjoint de
      fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means certified
      or registered mail or any other service that provides a record
      that a notice or document has been sent or delivered. ([service de
      messagerie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[consideration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[consideration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any amount that is
      payable by operation of law. ([contrepartie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[credit union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[credit union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 137(6) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([caisse de crédit]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means export from
      Canada. ([exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[government entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[government entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a department or agency of the government of
        Canada or of a province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a municipality;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an [[aboriginal
        government]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1)
        of the [Federal-Provincial Fiscal Arrangements
        Act](/eng/acts/F-7.8);

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a corporation all of the shares (except
        directors' qualifying shares) of the capital stock of which are
        owned by one or more persons each of which is

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a municipality, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a corporation described in this paragraph;
          or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a board or commission, established by Her
        Majesty in right of Canada or a province, that performs an
        administrative or regulatory function of government, or by a
        municipality, that performs an administrative or regulatory
        function of a municipality. ([entité
        gouvernementale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[guest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[guest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a particular person on a subject
      item means an individual that uses or enjoys the subject item and
      that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not deal at arm's length with the
        particular person;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is an employee of the particular person or of a
        person that does not deal at arm's length with the particular
        person; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} uses or enjoys the subject item, at the
        invitation of the particular person or a person referred to in
        paragraph (a) or (b), for no consideration or for nominal
        consideration. ([invité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[identification number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[identification number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a subject item
      means an identification number that is satisfactory to the
      Minister and is unique to the subject item. ([numéro
      d'identification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means import into
      Canada. ([importation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Indigenous governing body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Indigenous governing body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 2 of the [Department of Indigenous Services
      Act](/eng/acts/I-7.88). ([corps dirigeant
      autochtone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of any matter, means a
      judge of a superior court having jurisdiction in the province in
      which the matter arises or a judge of the Federal
      Court. ([juge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[military authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[military authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian
      Forces, within the meaning of section 14 of the [National Defence
      Act](/eng/acts/N-5), the Department of National Defence or a
      [[visiting force]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2
      of the [Visiting Forces Act](/eng/acts/V-2). ([autorité
      militaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of National
      Revenue. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[municipality]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[municipality]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an incorporated city, town, village,
        metropolitan authority, township, district, county or rural
        municipality or other incorporated municipal body however
        designated; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other local authority that the Minister may
        determine to be a municipality for the purposes of this
        Act. ([municipalité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in sections 75, 127
      and 149,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is appointed or employed in the
        administration or enforcement of this Act; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to imported goods that have not
        been released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), an
        [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1)
        of that Act. ([préposé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[passenger seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a seat on an aircraft
      other than a pilot seat. ([siège passager]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a partnership,
      a corporation, the estate or succession of a deceased individual,
      a trust, a joint venture, a government or a body that is a
      society, a union, a club, an association, a commission or another
      organization of any kind. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, of a deceased
      individual or the estate or succession of a deceased individual,
      means the executor of the individual's will, the liquidator of the
      individual's succession, the administrator of the estate or any
      person that is responsible under the appropriate law for the
      proper collection, administration, disposition and distribution of
      the assets of the estate or succession. ([représentant
      personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pilot seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pilot seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a flight engineer seat
      or a flight deck observer seat. ([siège destiné au
      pilote]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[police authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[police authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Royal Canadian Mounted Police, the Ontario
        Provincial Police, the Sûreté du Québec, the Canadian Coast
        Guard or a municipal or regional police force established in
        accordance with provincial legislation;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a government entity that is responsible for the
        preservation and maintenance of the public peace; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person. ([corps
        policier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a form or the manner of filing a
        form, authorized by the Minister;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the information to be given on
        or with a form, specified by the Minister; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, prescribed by regulation or
        determined in accordance with rules prescribed by
        regulation. ([Version anglaise
        seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[qualifying aircraft user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying aircraft user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      (other than a prescribed person) that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person that is an agent of Her Majesty in
        right of Canada or a province;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a municipality;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an Indigenous governing body;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a police authority;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a government entity that has as its primary
        responsibility the conduct of emergency medical response
        activities or emergency fire response activities;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a government entity that has as its primary
        responsibility the operation, management and maintenance of a
        hospital;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a person that has as its primary responsibility
        the operation, management and maintenance of a [[listed
        airport]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the
        [Air Travellers Security Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5);

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} NAV CANADA, a corporation incorporated on May
        26, 1995 under Part II of the [Canada Corporations
        Act](/eng/acts/C-1.8); or

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person. ([utilisateur admissible
        d'aéronef]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any material on which
      representations, in any form, of information or concepts are
      recorded or marked and that is capable of being read or understood
      by a person, a computer system or other
      device. ([registre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registered vendor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered vendor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a type of
      subject item, means a person that is registered under Division 5
      as a vendor in respect of that type of subject item. ([vendeur
      inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[select subject vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[select subject vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a subject vessel (other than a prescribed
        subject vessel) that is equipped with a bed, bunk, berth or
        similar sleeping amenity; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed subject vessel. ([navire assujetti
        désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subject aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subject aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an aircraft that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an [[aeroplane]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
        [[glider]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or
        [[helicopter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are defined
        in subsection 101.01(1) of the [Canadian Aviation
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-433), that has a date of
        manufacture after 2018 if the aircraft

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is equipped only with one or more pilot seats
          and cannot have any other seating configuration,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is equipped only with one or more pilot
          seats, or is not equipped with any seats, and cannot have a
          seating configuration, excluding pilot seats, of 40 or
          greater, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with one or more pilot seats
          and one or more passenger seats and has a seating
          configuration, excluding pilot seats, of 39 or fewer, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed aircraft,

      but does not include

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an aircraft that is designed and equipped for
        military activities,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an aircraft that is equipped for the carriage
        of goods only,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an aircraft

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is registered with a government before
          September 2022 otherwise than solely for a purpose incidental
          to its manufacture, offering for sale or transportation, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which a user of the aircraft
          has possession before September 2022,

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a subject vehicle, or

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed aircraft. ([aéronef
        assujetti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subject item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subject item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a subject aircraft, a
      subject vehicle or a subject vessel. ([bien
      assujetti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subject vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subject vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a motor vehicle that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is designed or adapted primarily to carry
          individuals on highways and streets,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a seating capacity of not more than 10
          individuals,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has a [[gross vehicle weight
          rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as that term is defined in
          subsection 2(1) of the [Motor Vehicle Safety
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1038), that is less
          than or equal to 3,856 kg,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} has a date of manufacture after 2018, and

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel with four or more
          wheels in contact with the ground, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed motor vehicle,

      but does not include

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an ambulance,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a hearse,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a motor vehicle that is clearly marked for
        policing activities,

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a motor vehicle that is clearly marked and
        equipped for emergency medical response activities or emergency
        fire response activities,

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a recreational vehicle that is designed or
        adapted to provide temporary residential accommodations, and is
        equipped with at least four of the following elements:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} cooking facilities,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a refrigerator or ice box,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a self-contained toilet,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a heating or air-conditioning system that
          can function independently of the vehicle engine,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a potable water supply system that includes a
          faucet and sink, and

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a 110-V to 125-V electric power supply, or a
          liquefied petroleum gas supply, that can function
          independently of the vehicle engine,

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a motor vehicle

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is registered before September 2022 with
          a government, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which possession was
          transferred to a user of the motor vehicle before September
          2022, or

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed motor vehicle. ([véhicule
        assujetti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subject vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subject vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vessel that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is designed or adapted for leisure,
          recreation or sport activities, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a date of manufacture after 2018, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed vessel,

      but does not include

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a [[floating home]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
        defined in subsection 123(1) of the [Excise Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/E-15),

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a vessel that is designed and equipped solely
        for

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} commercially catching, harvesting or
          transporting fish or other living marine resources, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} ferrying passengers or vehicles on a fixed
          schedule between two or more points,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a vessel that has sleeping facilities for more
        than 100 individuals who are not crew members,

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a vessel

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is registered with a government before
          September 2022, otherwise than solely for a purpose incidental
          to its manufacture, offering for sale or transportation, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which a user of the vessel has
          possession before September 2022,

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a subject vehicle or a subject aircraft, or

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed vessel. ([navire
        assujetti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in subsection 13(1),
      sections 15 and 16, subparagraph 18(2)(a)(iv) and paragraph
      42(1)(b), tax payable under this Act. ([taxe]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a boat, ship or craft that is
      designed, or is capable of being used, solely or partly for
      navigation in, on, through or immediately above water, without
      regard to the method or lack of
      propulsion. ([navire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In or in respect of Subdivision B of Division 2,
  [[Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of administration or enforcement
of this Act

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty, a
reference in this Act to the administration or enforcement of this Act
includes the collection of any amount payable under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person resident in Canada

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
Division 2 of Part 2 and paragraph 21(6)(e), a person is deemed to be
resident in Canada at any time

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corporation, if the corporation is
  incorporated or continued in Canada and not continued elsewhere;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a partnership, a joint venture, an
  unincorporated society, a club, an association or an organization, or
  a branch thereof, if the member or participant, or a majority of the
  members or participants, having management and control thereof is or
  are resident in Canada at that time;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a labour union, if the labour union is
  carrying on activities as such in Canada and has a local union or
  branch in Canada at that time; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an individual, if the individual is
  deemed under any of paragraphs 250(1)(b) to (f) of the [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) to be resident in Canada at that time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arm's length

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} related persons are deemed not to deal with each
    other at arm's length;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} associated persons are deemed not to deal with each
    other at arm's length; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is a question of fact whether persons not
    related to, or not associated with, each other are, at any
    particular time, dealing with each other at arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, persons are related to
  each other if they are related persons within the meaning of
  subsections 251(2) to (6) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),
  except that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference in those subsections to "corporation"
    is to be read as a reference to "corporation or partnership"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference in those subsections to "shares of the
    capital stock of a corporation" or "shareholders" is, in respect of
    a partnership, to be read as a reference to "rights in a
    partnership" or "partners", respectively.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related persons --- partnership

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a member of a
  partnership is deemed to be related to the partnership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Associated persons

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A particular corporation is associated with another
  corporation for the purposes of this Act if, by reason of subsections
  256(1) to (6) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the particular
  corporation is associated with the other corporation for the purposes
  of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporations controlled by same person
  or group

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person other than a corporation is associated with
  a particular corporation for the purposes of this Act if the
  particular corporation is controlled by the person or by a group of
  persons of which the person is a member and each of whom is associated
  with each of the others.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partnership or trust

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person is associated
  with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a partnership if the total of the shares of the
    profits of the partnership to which the person and all other persons
    who are associated with the person are entitled is more than half of
    the total profits of the partnership, or would be more than half of
    the total profits of the partnership if it had profits; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trust if the total of the values of the interests
    in the trust of the person and all other persons who are associated
    with the person is more than half of the total value of all
    interests in the trust.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Association with third person

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person is associated
  with another person if each of them is associated with the same third
  person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Associated persons --- partnership

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a member of a
  partnership is deemed to be associated with the partnership.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Negative amounts

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** Except as specifically
otherwise provided, if an amount or a number is required under this Act
to be determined or calculated by or in accordance with an algebraic
formula and the amount or number when so determined or calculated would,
in the absence of this section, be a negative amount or number, it is
deemed to be zero.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- subject item

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act, a vendor sells a subject item to a purchaser if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vendor transfers ownership of the subject item
    to the purchaser by way of sale under an agreement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is delivered or made available in
    Canada in relation to the agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partial ownership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a particular person
  transfers ownership of a subject item to another person even if, at
  the time ownership is transferred to the other person, the particular
  person retains partial ownership or transfers partial ownership to any
  third person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security interest --- not a sale

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act and despite subsection
  (1), if, under an agreement entered into in respect of a debt or
  obligation, a person transfers a subject item or an interest in a
  subject item for the purpose of securing payment of the debt or
  performance of the obligation, the transfer is deemed not to be a sale
  of the subject item and the transferee is deemed not to be an owner of
  the subject item only because of the transfer, and if, on payment of
  the debt or performance of the obligation or the forgiveness of the
  debt or obligation, the subject item or interest is retransferred, the
  retransfer of the subject item or interest is deemed not to be a sale
  of a subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When sale completed

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), for the purposes of this
  Act, the sale of a subject item to a purchaser is completed at the
  earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time at which possession of the subject item is
    transferred to the purchaser or to another person, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time at which ownership of the subject item is
    transferred to the purchaser.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When sale completed --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if prescribed
  conditions are met in respect of the sale of a subject item to a
  purchaser, the sale is completed at the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed sale

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, except if prescribed
  circumstances exist, if ownership of a subject item is transferred in
  any manner otherwise than by way of sale from a particular person to
  another person and if the subject item is delivered or made available
  in Canada, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the particular person is deemed to sell the subject
    item to the other person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the particular person is deemed to be the vendor in
    respect of the sale and the other person is deemed to be the
    purchaser in respect of the sale;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the sale is deemed to be completed at the earlier
    of the time at which the possession of the subject item is
    transferred and the time at which ownership of the subject item is
    transferred; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the value of the consideration paid for the sale of
    the subject item is deemed to be equal to the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the time
      at which the sale is completed as determined under paragraph (c),
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed amount.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Improvement to subject item

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), for the purposes of this Act, an improvement in
  respect of a subject item is the provision of property or a service in
  any manner, including by way of sale, transfer, barter, exchange,
  licence, rental, lease, gift or disposition, that is a provision of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tangible personal property that is installed in or
    on, or is affixed to, the subject item;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a service that modifies the subject item and is
    physically performed in respect of the subject item; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed property or service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded improvements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the provision of
  property or a service is deemed not to be an improvement in respect of
  a subject item if it is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provision of a repair, cleaning or maintenance
    service in respect of the subject item;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provision of tangible personal property to
    replace other tangible personal property that is a part of the
    subject item and that is damaged, defective or non-functioning;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vehicle,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the provision of tangible personal property that
      is, or a service that is in respect of,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a child safety seating system or a child safety
        restraint system, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a trailer or camper, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the provision of tangible personal property or a
      service that specially equips or adapts the subject vehicle

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} for its use by or in transporting an individual
        using a wheelchair, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} with an auxiliary driving control to facilitate
        the operation of the vehicle by an individual with a disability;
        or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the provision of a prescribed property or service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When improvement completed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), for the purposes of this
  Act, an improvement in respect of a subject item is completed at

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the improvement is the provision of tangible
    personal property that is installed in or on, or is affixed to, the
    subject item, the time at which the installation of the tangible
    personal property is physically completed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the improvement is the provision of a service
    that is physically performed in respect of the subject item, the
    time at which the performance of the service is physically
    completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When improvement completed ---
  regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if prescribed
  conditions are met in respect of an improvement in respect of a
  subject item, the improvement is completed at the prescribed time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Price threshold

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, the price threshold in respect of a subject item is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vehicle, \$100,000;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft, \$100,000; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vessel, \$250,000.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, a [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  includes a profession, calling, trade, manufacture or undertaking of
  any kind whatever and any activity engaged in on a regular or
  continuous basis that involves the provision of property by way of
  lease, licence or similar arrangement, but does not include an office
  or employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where flights originate and terminate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a flight of a
  subject aircraft originates at the location of the
  [[take-off]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as that term is defined in
  subsection 101.01(1) of the [Canadian Aviation
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-433), of the subject aircraft and
  terminates at the location of the [[landing]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
  as that term is defined in that subsection, of the subject aircraft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying flight

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, except if
  prescribed circumstances exist, a subject aircraft is used for a
  flight that is a qualifying flight if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of the flight is to provide

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a [[scheduled service]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
      defined in subsection 3(1) of the [Transportation Information
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-334),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an air ambulance service,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an aerial fire fighting service,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an aerial forest fire management service,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} an aerial search and rescue operation,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} an aerial transportation service for the
      retrieval and transportation of organs for human transplant,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} an aerial weather altering service,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} an aerial survey service,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} an aerial construction service,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} an aerial spraying or spreading service,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} an air flight training service, or

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} for the carriage of goods only;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is the case that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all of the passenger seats
      on the flight are offered for sale on a seat-by-seat basis to the
      general public, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all or substantially all of the passengers on
      the flight are individuals that deal at arm's length with

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the person that operates the subject aircraft
        for the flight,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} each person that is an owner of the subject
        aircraft, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in cases where one or more of those passenger
        seats are offered for sale by a person other than a person
        referred to in clause (A) or (B), that other person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the flight originates or terminates in a remote
    community listed in the schedule;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the flight is conducted in the course of a business
    of an owner of the subject aircraft (other than a business without a
    reasonable expectation of profit) and otherwise than for the
    leisure, recreation, sport or other enjoyment of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an owner of the subject aircraft,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a guest of an owner of the subject aircraft on
      the subject aircraft, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} another person that has the right to use the
      subject aircraft under a lease, licence or similar arrangement or
      a guest of the other person on the subject aircraft; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying subject aircraft

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, except in prescribed
  circumstances, a subject aircraft is a qualifying subject aircraft of
  a person at a particular time that is on a particular day if the
  person is an owner of the subject aircraft at the particular time and
  the amount determined by the following formula is greater than or
  equal to 0.9:

  ::: {#1363337 .Subsection}
  (A + B + C) / (D + E + F)

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the particular time is the time at which the
        person acquires ownership of the subject aircraft, zero, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the total of all amounts,
        each of which is a duration of time that the subject aircraft
        was used for a flight that was a qualifying flight and that
        originated or terminated at a location in Canada during the
        period that ends on the particular day and begins on the later
        of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person became an owner
          of the subject aircraft, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day that is one year before the
          particular day;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is a duration of time
      for which it can reasonably be expected that the subject aircraft
      will be used for a flight
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is a qualifying flight,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that originates or terminates at a location in
        Canada during the period that begins on the day after the
        particular day and that ends on the day that is one year after
        the particular day, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} throughout which the person is an owner of the
        subject aircraft;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the particular time is the time at which the
        person acquires ownership of the subject aircraft, zero, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the total of all amounts,
        each of which is a duration of time that the subject aircraft
        was used for a flight that originated or terminated at a
        location in Canada during the period that ends on the particular
        day and begins on the later of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person acquired
          ownership of the subject aircraft, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day that is one year before the
          particular day;

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is a duration of time
      for which it can reasonably be expected that the subject aircraft
      will be used for a flight
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that originates or terminates at a location in
        Canada during the period that begins on the day after the
        particular day and that ends on the day that is one year after
        the particular day, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} throughout which the person is an owner of the
        subject aircraft; and

  [F]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying subject aircraft ---
  regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a subject aircraft is a
  qualifying subject aircraft of a person at any time if prescribed
  conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where vessel journeys originate and
terminate

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, a journey by a subject vessel originates
  at the location at which any of the following activities occur and
  terminates at the location where the subject vessel is next stopped
  and at which any of the following activities occur:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} individuals embark on or disembark from the subject
    vessel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} goods are loaded onto or removed from the subject
    vessel; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the subject vessel is stopped to allow for its
    servicing or refuelling or for emergency or safety purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use in Canada --- vessel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, if a journey by a
  subject vessel originates or terminates at a location in Canada, the
  subject vessel is deemed to be used in Canada for the duration of the
  entire journey.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying activities --- vessel

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, except in
  prescribed circumstances, a subject vessel (other than a select
  subject vessel) is used in Canada in the course of a qualifying
  activity at any time if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject vessel is used in Canada at that time
    otherwise than for the leisure, recreation, sport or other enjoyment
    of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an owner of the subject vessel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a guest of an owner of the subject vessel on the
      subject vessel, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} another person that has the right to use the
      subject vessel under a lease, licence or similar arrangement or a
      guest of the other person on the subject vessel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying subject vessel

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, except in prescribed
  circumstances, a subject vessel (other than a select subject vessel)
  is a qualifying subject vessel of a person at a particular time that
  is on a particular day if the person is an owner of the subject vessel
  at the particular time and the amount determined by the following
  formula is greater than or equal to 0.9:

  ::: {#1363377 .Subsection}
  (A + B + C) / (D + E + F)

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the particular time is the time at which the
        person acquires ownership of the subject vessel, zero, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the total of all amounts,
        each of which is a duration of time that the subject vessel was
        used in Canada in the course of a qualifying activity during the
        period that ends on the particular day and begins on the later
        of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person acquired
          ownership of the subject vessel, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day that is one year before the
          particular day;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is a duration of time
      for which it can reasonably be expected that the subject vessel
      will be used in Canada in the course of a qualifying activity
      during the period that begins on the day after the particular day
      and that ends on
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it can reasonably be expected that the
        person will transfer ownership of the subject vessel before the
        day that is one year after the particular day, the day on which
        it can reasonably be expected that the person will transfer
        ownership of the subject vessel, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the day that is one year
        after the particular day;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the particular time is the time at which the
        person acquires ownership of the subject vessel, zero, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the total of all amounts,
        each of which is a duration of time that the subject vessel was
        used in Canada during the period that ends on the particular day
        and begins on the later of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person acquired
          ownership of the subject vessel, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day that is one year before the
          particular day;

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is a duration of time
      for which it can reasonably be expected that the subject vessel
      will be used in Canada during the period that begins on the day
      after the particular day and that ends on
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it can reasonably be expected that the
        person will transfer ownership of the subject vessel before the
        day that is one year after the particular day, the day on which
        it can reasonably be expected that the person will transfer
        ownership of the subject vessel to another person, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the day that is one year
        after the particular day; and

  [F]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying subject vessel ---
  regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a subject vessel is a
  qualifying subject vessel of a person at any time if prescribed
  conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of vehicle

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, a subject vehicle is registered with a
  government if it is registered with, or licensed by, that government
  for the purposes of permitting that subject vehicle to travel on
  public roads within the jurisdiction of that government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of aircraft

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a subject aircraft is
  registered with a government if it is registered with, or licensed by,
  that government for the purposes of permitting that subject aircraft
  to fly within the jurisdiction of that government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of vessel

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a subject vessel is
  registered with a government if it is registered with, or licensed by,
  that government for the purposes of permitting that subject vessel to
  navigate within the jurisdiction of that government.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Consideration and Retail Value]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363406 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Subdivision.

  [[money]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[money]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any currency, cheque,
      promissory note, letter of credit, draft, traveller's cheque, bill
      of exchange, postal note, money order, postal remittance and other
      similar instrument, whether Canadian or foreign, but does not
      include currency the fair market value of which exceeds its stated
      value as legal tender in the country of issuance or currency that
      is supplied or held for its numismatic
      value. ([argent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial levy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial levy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a tax, duty or fee imposed under an Act of the
        legislature of a province in respect of the supply, consumption
        or use of property or a service, other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a tax, duty or fee that is included in the
          [Taxes, Duties and Fees (GST/HST)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-34) or that would be so
          included if paragraph 3(b) of those Regulations were read
          without reference to subparagraph (iv) of that paragraph, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed tax, duty, fee or amount; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed tax, duty or fee. ([prélèvement
        provincial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a provision of property or a
      service in any manner, including sale, transfer, barter, exchange,
      licence, rental, lease, gift or
      disposition. ([fourniture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When supply is made

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Subdivision, a supply is
  made at the time at which

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the supply is a sale of a subject item, the sale
    is completed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the supply is an improvement in respect of a
    subject item, the improvement is completed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the supply is made in connection with a sale of
    a subject item and is not an improvement in respect of the subject
    item, the sale is completed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the supply is made in connection with an
    improvement in respect of a subject item and is not an improvement
    in respect of the subject item, the improvement is completed; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the supply is made for the
    purposes of Part IX of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When supply is made --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), for the purposes of this
  Subdivision, if prescribed conditions are met in respect of a supply,
  the supply is made at the prescribed time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of consideration

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Subdivision, for the
  purposes of this Act, the value of the consideration, or any part of
  the consideration, for a supply is deemed to be equal to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the consideration or that part is expressed in
    money, the amount of the money; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the fair market value of the
    consideration or that part at the time at which the supply was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combined consideration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4), for the purposes
  of this Act, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consideration is paid for a supply and other
    consideration is paid for one or more other supplies or matters, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the consideration for one of the supplies or
    matters exceeds the consideration that would be reasonable if the
    other supply were not made or the other matter were not provided,

  the consideration for each of the supplies and matters is deemed to be
  that part of the total of all amounts, each of which is consideration
  for one of those supplies or matters, that may reasonably be
  attributed to each of those supplies and matters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration for sale includes fees

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if consideration is
  paid for the sale of a subject item and other consideration is paid
  for one or more other supplies or matters (other than an improvement
  in respect of the subject item) and the other consideration represents
  a fee or other similar charge in respect of the subject item or the
  sale, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other supplies and matters are deemed to form
    part of the sale; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the other consideration is to be included in the
    consideration for the sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration for improvement includes
  fees

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if consideration is
  paid for an improvement in respect of a subject item and other
  consideration is paid for one or more other supplies or matters (other
  than a sale of the subject item) and the other consideration
  represents a fee or other similar charge in respect of the
  improvement, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other supplies and matters are deemed to form
    part of the improvement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the other consideration is to be included in the
    consideration for the improvement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incidental supplies

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if a particular
  property or service is supplied together with any other property or
  service for a single consideration and if it may reasonably be
  regarded that the provision of the other property or service is
  incidental to the provision of the particular property or service, the
  other property or service is deemed to form part of the particular
  property or service so supplied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-arm's length supplies

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if a supply of property
  or a service is made between persons not dealing with each other at
  arm's length for consideration less than the fair market value of the
  property or service at the time at which the supply is made, the
  consideration paid for the supply is deemed to be equal to the fair
  market value of the property or service at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nominal consideration

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if a supply of property
  or a service is made for no consideration or nominal consideration,
  the consideration paid for the supply is deemed to be equal to the
  fair market value of the property or service at the time at which the
  supply is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Improvement by way of lease, etc.

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if an improvement in
  respect of a subject item is provided by way of lease, licence or
  similar arrangement and the improvement is the provision of tangible
  personal property, the consideration paid for the improvement is
  deemed to be equal to the fair market value of the improvement at the
  time at which the improvement is completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value in Canadian currency

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if the consideration
  for a supply is expressed in a foreign currency, the value of the
  consideration is to be computed on the basis of the value of that
  foreign currency in Canadian currency on the day that the supply is
  made, or on such other day as is acceptable to the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Levies included in consideration

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, the consideration for a supply of property or a service includes

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any tax, duty or fee imposed under an Act of
  Parliament that is payable or collectible in respect of the supply,
  production or importation of the property or service, other than tax
  under this Act or Part IX of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) that
  is payable in respect of the property or service;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any provincial levy that is payable or collectible in
  respect of the supply or production of the property or service; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other amount that is collectible in respect of
  the supply or production of the property or service under an Act of
  the legislature of a province and that is equal to, or is collectible
  on account of or in lieu of, a provincial levy.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retail value of subject item

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, the retail value of a subject item at any time is the amount
determined by the formula

::: {#1363461 .Section}
A + B + C + D

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the fair market value of the subject item at that time;

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all amounts, each of which is a fee or charge in
    respect of the transportation or freight of the subject item but
    only to the extent that the amount is not included in the
    determination of A;

[C]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount described in
    any of the following paragraphs but only to the extent that each
    amount is not included in the determination of A:
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a tax, duty or fee imposed under an Act of
      Parliament that is payable or collectible in respect of a supply
      of the subject item or in respect of the production or importation
      of the subject item, other than tax under this Act or Part IX of
      the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) that is payable in respect of
      the subject item,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a provincial levy that is payable or collectible
      in respect of a supply of the subject item or in respect of the
      production of the subject item, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} another amount that is collectible in respect of
      a supply of the subject item or in respect of the production of
      the subject item under an Act of the legislature of a province and
      that is equal to, or is collectible on account of or in lieu of, a
      provincial levy; and

[D]{.dfn}
:   is a prescribed amount.
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Her Majesty]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363470 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Her Majesty

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on
Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Application of Tax]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1363473 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Tax on Sale]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363474 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- sale of subject item

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a vendor
  sells a subject item to a purchaser and the taxable amount of the
  subject item exceeds the price threshold in respect of the subject
  item, tax in respect of the subject item is payable to Her Majesty in
  right of Canada in the amount determined under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for tax --- vendor or
  purchaser

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a subject
  item sold by a vendor to a purchaser is payable by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser, in the case where the vendor is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada or a
      province,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an Indigenous governing body,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a person entitled to tax relief privileges under
      the [Foreign Missions and International Organizations
      Act](/eng/acts/F-29.4) for the tax payable under subsection 165(1)
      of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) in respect of the sale, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vendor, in any other case.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  time at which the sale is completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (5) to (7), the taxable amount
  of a subject item sold by a vendor to a purchaser is, for the purposes
  of this section and for the purposes of determining under section 34
  the amount of tax payable under this section, the amount determined by
  the formula

  ::: {#1363491 .Subsection}
  A + B + C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the value of the consideration for the sale of the subject
      item;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is the value of the
      consideration for an improvement in respect of the subject item
      that is provided by the vendor, or a person that does not deal at
      arm's length with the vendor, in connection with the sale of the
      subject item, but only to the extent that the amount is not
      included in the determination of A; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- lease by non-arm's
  length purchaser

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7), if a vendor sells a
  subject item to a purchaser that does not deal at arm's length with
  the vendor, if the purchaser and another person enter into a lease,
  licence or similar arrangement (in this subsection referred to as the
  "lease agreement") that provides the other person with the right to
  use the subject item for a period of at least six months and if the
  lease agreement is entered into in connection with the sale, the
  taxable amount of the subject item is, for the purposes of this
  section and for the purposes of determining under section 34 the
  amount of tax payable under this section, the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1363498 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the greatest of
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the value of the consideration for the sale,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
        time at which the other person first has the right to use the
        subject item under the lease agreement, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
        time at which possession of the subject item is transferred to
        the other person under the lease agreement; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- lease to vendor by
  purchaser

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7), if a vendor sells a
  subject item to a purchaser, if the purchaser and the vendor enter
  into a lease, licence or similar arrangement (in this subsection
  referred to as the "lease agreement") that provides the vendor with
  the right to use the subject item for a period of at least six months
  and if the lease agreement is entered into in connection with the
  sale, the taxable amount of the subject item is, for the purposes of
  this section and for the purposes of determining under section 34 the
  amount of tax payable under this section, the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1363507 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the greater of
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the value of the consideration for the sale,
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
        time at which the vendor first has the right to use the subject
        item under the lease agreement; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- registered vendor of
vehicles

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  tax under section 18 in respect of a subject vehicle sold by a vendor
  to a purchaser is not payable if an exemption certificate applies in
  respect of the sale of the subject vehicle in accordance with section
  36 and if the vendor is a registered vendor in respect of subject
  vehicles or a prescribed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- previously
  registered vehicle

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 18 in respect of a subject
  vehicle sold by a vendor to a purchaser is not payable if the subject
  vehicle has been registered with the Government of Canada or a
  province except if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle was registered only because of
    the sale and has never otherwise been registered with the Government
    of Canada or a province; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vendor

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province,
      an agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province or an
      Indigenous governing body,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} imported the subject vehicle, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} did not pay tax under section 20 in respect of
      the importation of the subject vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- certain vehicles

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 18 in respect of a subject
  vehicle sold by a vendor to a purchaser is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is equipped for policing
    activities and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser is a police authority or a military
      authority, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is the case that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser and a police authority or
        military authority enter into an agreement that is a lease,
        licence or similar arrangement,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the agreement is entered into at or before the
        time at which the sale is completed,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the police authority or military authority has
        the right to use the subject vehicle for a period of at least
        six months under the agreement,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser transfers possession of the
        subject vehicle to the police authority or military authority
        under the agreement, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser provides to the vendor, and the
        vendor retains, evidence satisfactory to the Minister that the
        conditions in clauses (A) to (D) are met in respect of the
        subject vehicle; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is equipped for military
    activities and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser is a military authority, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is the case that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser and a military authority enter
        into an agreement that is a lease, licence or similar
        arrangement,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the agreement is entered into at or before the
        time at which the sale is completed,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the military authority has the right to use the
        subject vehicle for a period of at least six months under the
        agreement,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser transfers possession of the
        subject vehicle to the military authority under the agreement,
        and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser provides to the vendor, and the
        vendor retains, evidence satisfactory to the Minister that the
        conditions in clauses (A) to (D) are met in respect of the
        subject vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate for aircraft or
  vessel

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 18 in respect of a subject
  aircraft or subject vessel sold by a vendor to a purchaser is not
  payable if an exemption certificate applies in respect of the sale in
  accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax certificate for aircraft or vessel

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 18 in respect of a subject
  aircraft or subject vessel sold by a vendor to a purchaser is not
  payable if a tax certificate in respect of the subject aircraft or
  subject vessel is in effect in accordance with section 37 at the time
  at which the sale is completed.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Tax on Importation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363547 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- importation into Canada

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a person that
  is liable under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty on an
  imported subject item, or that would be so liable if the subject item
  were subject to duty, must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada tax
  in respect of the subject item in the amount determined under section
  34 if the taxable amount of the subject item exceeds the price
  threshold in respect of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the taxable amount of a
  subject item that is imported is, for the purposes of this section and
  for the purposes of determining under section 34 the amount of tax
  payable under this section, the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1363553 .Subsection}
  A + B + C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the value of the subject item, as it would be determined under
      the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for the purposes of
      calculating duties imposed on the subject item at a percentage
      rate, whether the subject item is in fact subject to duty;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount of all duties and taxes, if any, payable on the
      subject item under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the
      [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) (other than Part IX of that Act),
      the [Special Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) or any other law
      relating to customs; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, tax under this section in
  respect of a subject item is to be paid and collected under the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), and interest and penalties are to be
  imposed, calculated, paid and collected under that Act, as if the tax
  were a customs duty levied on the subject item under the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and, for those purposes, the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies with any modifications that the
  circumstances require.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- registered vendor

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  tax under section 20 in respect of a subject item that is imported is
  not payable if the subject item is imported by a registered vendor in
  respect of that type of subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- previously
  registered vehicles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 20 in respect of a subject
  vehicle that is imported is not payable if the subject vehicle has
  been registered with the Government of Canada or a province before the
  importation unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registration was done in connection with the
    importation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle has never otherwise been
    registered with the Government of Canada or a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- certain vehicles

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 20 in respect of a subject
  vehicle that is imported is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is equipped for policing
    activities and imported by a police authority or a military
    authority; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is equipped for military
    activities and imported by a military authority.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- tax certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 20 in respect of a subject
  aircraft or subject vessel is not payable if a tax certificate in
  respect of the subject aircraft or subject vessel is in effect in
  accordance with section 37 at the time at which the tax would become
  payable in the absence of this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- special import
  certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 20 in respect of a subject
  aircraft or subject vessel (other than a select subject vessel) that
  is imported is not payable if, at the time at which the tax would
  become payable in the absence of this subsection, a special import
  certificate in respect of the importation is in effect in accordance
  with section 38.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- special cases

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The tax under section 20 in respect of a subject item
  that is imported is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is classified under heading No.
    98.01 or tariff item No. 9802.00.00 or 9803.00.00 of the schedule to
    the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), to the extent that the
    subject item is not subject to duty under that Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is imported for the sole purpose
    of maintenance, overhaul or repair of the subject item in Canada and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} neither title to, nor beneficial use of, the
      subject item is intended to pass, or passes, to a person in Canada
      while the subject item is in Canada, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is exported as soon after the
      maintenance, overhaul or repair is completed as is reasonable
      having regard to the circumstances surrounding the importation
      and, where applicable, to the normal business practice of the
      importer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is the case that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is a foreign-based conveyance,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the importation of the subject item was
      non-taxable by reason of the reference to heading No. 98.01 of the
      schedule to the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) in
      paragraph (a) but the subject item is diverted solely for
      maintenance, overhaul or repair in Canada,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} neither title to, nor beneficial use of, the
      subject item is intended to pass, or passes, to a person in Canada
      while the subject item is in Canada, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is exported as soon after the
      maintenance, overhaul or repair is completed as is reasonable
      having regard to the circumstances surrounding the importation
      and, where applicable, to the normal business practice of the
      importer;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is a subject vessel imported in
    circumstances where customs duties have been removed under
    subsection 7(1) of the [Vessel Duties Reduction or Removal
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-90-304);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is a subject vehicle that is
    imported temporarily by an individual resident in Canada and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the subject item was last provided in the course
      of a vehicle rental business to the individual by way of lease,
      licence or similar arrangement under which continuous possession
      or use of the subject item is provided for a period of less than
      180 days,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} immediately before the importation, the
      individual was outside Canada for an uninterrupted period of at
      least 48 hours, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is exported within 30 days
      after the importation; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the subject item would be classified under tariff
    item No. 9802.00.00 of the schedule to the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) to the extent that the subject item
    would not be subject to duty under that Act if the definition
    [[conveyance]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the [Temporary
    Importation of Conveyances by Residents of Canada
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-82-840) were read as follows:

    > ::: ReadAsText
    >
    > [[conveyance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
    >
    > :   [[conveyance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any vehicle,
    >     aircraft, water-borne craft or other contrivance that is used
    >     to move persons or goods;
    > :::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[determination of the tax
status]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[determination of the tax status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  of a subject item means a determination, re-determination or further
  re-determination that tax under this Subdivision is, or is not,
  payable in respect of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) --- determination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) to (6), the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) (other than subsections 67(2) and (3) and
  sections 68 and 70) and the regulations made under that Act apply,
  with any modifications that the circumstances require, to the
  determination of the tax status of a subject item for the purposes of
  this Subdivision as if it were the determination, re-determination or
  further re-determination, as the case requires, of the tariff
  classification of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) --- appraisal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the
  regulations made under that Act apply, with any modifications that the
  circumstances require, to the appraisal, re-appraisal or further
  re-appraisal of the value of a subject item for the purposes of this
  Subdivision as if it were the appraisal, re-appraisal or further
  re-appraisal, as the case requires, of the value for duty of the
  subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeals of determination of tax status

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In applying the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to a
  determination of the tax status of a subject item, the references in
  that Act to the "Canadian International Trade Tribunal" are to be read
  as references to the "Tax Court of Canada".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- this Act and [Tax Court
  of Canada Act](/eng/acts/T-2)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The provisions of this Act and of the [Tax Court of
  Canada Act](/eng/acts/T-2) that apply to an appeal taken under section
  100 apply, with any modifications that the circumstances require, to
  an appeal taken under subsection 67(1) of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) from a decision of the President of the Canada
  Border Services Agency made under section 60 or 61 of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) in a determination of the tax status of a
  subject item as if the decision of the President were a confirmation
  of an assessment or a reassessment made by the Minister under
  subsection 97(8) or (9) as a consequence of a notice of objection
  filed under subsection 97(1) by the person to which the President is
  required to give notice under section 60 or 61 of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), as the case may be, of the decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- appraisal or re-appraisal

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If, because of an appraisal, a re-appraisal or a
  further re-appraisal of the value of a subject item or a determination
  of the tax status of a subject item, it is determined that the amount
  that was paid as tax under this Subdivision in respect of the subject
  item exceeds the amount of tax that is required under this Subdivision
  to be paid in respect of the subject item and a refund of the excess
  would be given under paragraph 59(3)(b) or 65(1)(b) of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) if the tax under this Subdivision in respect of
  the subject item were a customs duty in respect of the subject item
  levied under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), a rebate of the
  excess is to be paid, subject to section 46, to the person that paid
  the excess, and the provisions of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)
  that relate to the payment of such refunds and interest on such
  refunds apply, with any modifications that the circumstances require,
  as if the rebate of the excess were a refund of duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 69 of [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 46, section 69 of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies, with any modifications that the
  circumstances require, if an appeal in respect of the value of a
  subject item or a determination of the tax status of a subject item is
  taken for the purpose of determining whether tax under this
  Subdivision in respect of the subject item is payable or of
  determining the amount of such tax.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Tax in Other Circumstances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363612 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- registration of registered
vendor's vehicle

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a registered
  vendor in respect of subject vehicles is an owner of a subject vehicle
  at a particular time, if the subject vehicle is registered at the
  particular time with the Government of Canada or a province, if the
  subject vehicle has never otherwise been registered with the
  Government of Canada or a province and if the taxable amount of the
  subject vehicle exceeds the price threshold in respect of the subject
  vehicle, the registered vendor must pay to Her Majesty in right of
