  Canada tax in respect of the subject vehicle in the amount determined
  under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  particular time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a
  registration of a subject vehicle owned by a registered vendor is not
  payable if the registration of the subject vehicle is done only
  because

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is sold by the registered
    vendor to a purchaser; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the right to use the subject vehicle is provided by
    the registered vendor to a person under a lease, licence or similar
    arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the taxable amount of a
  subject vehicle that is owned by a registered vendor is, for the
  purposes of this section and for the purposes of determining under
  section 34 the amount of tax payable under this section, the amount
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#1363624 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the greater of
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject vehicle at the
        time at which the subject vehicle is registered with the
        Government of Canada or a province, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject vehicle at the
        time at which the registered vendor first uses the subject
        vehicle; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject vehicle in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- lease of subject vehicle

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a registered
  vendor in respect of subject vehicles is an owner of a subject
  vehicle, if the registered vendor provides the right to use the
  subject vehicle to another person under a lease, licence or similar
  arrangement, if the subject vehicle has not previously been registered
  with the Government of Canada or a province otherwise than in
  connection with the lease, licence or similar arrangement and if the
  taxable amount of the subject vehicle exceeds the price threshold in
  respect of the subject vehicle, the registered vendor must pay to Her
  Majesty in right of Canada tax in respect of the subject vehicle in
  the amount determined under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  time at which the other person first has the right to use the subject
  item under the lease, licence or similar arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a subject
  vehicle that is owned by a registered vendor and in respect of which
  the right to use the subject item is provided to another person under
  a lease, licence or similar arrangement is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is equipped for policing
    activities and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the other person is a police authority or
      military authority,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the other person has the right to use the
      subject item for a period of at least six months under the lease,
      licence or similar arrangement,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the registered vendor transfers possession of
      the subject vehicle to the other person under the lease, licence
      or similar arrangement, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the registered vendor retains evidence
      satisfactory to the Minister that the conditions in
      subparagraphs (i) to (iii) are met in respect of the subject item;
      or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject vehicle is equipped for military
    activities and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the other person is a military authority,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the other person has the right to use the
      subject item for a period of at least six months under the lease,
      licence or similar arrangement,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the registered vendor transfers possession of
      the subject vehicle to the other person under the lease, licence
      or similar arrangement, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the registered vendor retains evidence
      satisfactory to the Minister that the conditions in
      subparagraphs (i) to (iii) are met in respect of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the taxable amount of a
  subject vehicle that is owned by a registered vendor and in respect of
  which the right to use the subject item is provided to another person
  under a lease, licence or similar arrangement is, for the purposes of
  this section and for the purposes of determining under section 34 the
  amount of tax payable under this section, the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1363651 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the greater of
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject vehicle at the
        time at which possession of the subject vehicle is first
        transferred to the other person under the lease, licence or
        similar arrangement, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject vehicle at the
        time at which the other person first has the right to use the
        subject vehicle under the lease, license or similar arrangement;
        and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- lease of aircraft or vessel

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a particular
  person is an owner of a subject item that is a subject aircraft or
  subject vessel, if the particular person provides the right to use the
  subject item to another person under a lease, licence or similar
  arrangement and if the taxable amount of the subject item exceeds the
  price threshold in respect of the subject item, the particular person
  must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada tax in respect of the
  subject item in the amount determined under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  time at which the other person first has the right to use the subject
  item under the lease, licence or similar arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a subject
  item that is owned by a particular person and in respect of which the
  right to use the subject item is provided to another person under a
  lease, licence or similar arrangement is not payable if, at the time
  at which the other person first has the right to use the subject item
  under a lease, licence or similar arrangement

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vessel (other than a
    select subject vessel), the subject item is a qualifying subject
    vessel of the particular person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is a qualifying subject aircraft
      of the particular person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each person that is an owner of the subject item
      is a qualifying aircraft user, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the other person is a qualifying aircraft user;
      or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a tax certificate in respect of the subject item is
    in effect in accordance with section 37.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the taxable amount of a
  subject item that is owned by a particular person and in respect of
  which the right to use the subject item is provided to another person
  under a lease, licence or similar arrangement is, for the purposes of
  this section and for the purposes of determining under section 34 the
  amount of tax payable under this section, the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1363674 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the greater of
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
        time at which possession of the subject item is first
        transferred to the other person under the lease, licence or
        similar arrangement, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
        time at which the other person first has the right to use the
        subject item under the lease, license or similar arrangement;
        and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- use of aircraft or vessel

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a person is
  an owner at a particular time of a subject item that is a subject
  aircraft or subject vessel, if the subject item is used in Canada at
  the particular time and if the taxable amount of the subject item
  exceeds the price threshold in respect of the subject item, the person
  must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada tax in respect of the
  subject item in the amount determined under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  particular time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a subject
  item that is used in Canada by a person at a particular time is not
  payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vessel (other than a
    select subject vessel), the subject item is a qualifying subject
    vessel of the person at the particular time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a registered vendor in respect of
    that type of subject item and the use of the subject item by the
    person is reasonably necessary or incidental to the manufacture,
    offering for sale or transportation of the subject item;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft, at the
    particular time

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is a qualifying subject
      aircraft, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each person that is an owner of the subject
      aircraft is a qualifying aircraft user;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a tax certificate in respect of the subject item is
    in effect at the particular time in accordance with section 37;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the person imported the subject item before the
    particular time and tax under section 20 in respect of the
    importation was not payable by the person because of the application
    of subsection 21(6); or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} tax in respect of the subject item became payable
    under section 27 or 28 by the person at or before the particular
    time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the taxable amount of a
  subject item that is used in Canada at a particular time is, for the
  purposes of this section and for the purposes of determining under
  section 34 the amount of tax payable under this section, the amount
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#1363699 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the retail value of the subject item at the particular time;
      and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- ceasing to be registered vendor

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a person
  ceases to be a registered vendor in respect of a type of subject item
  at a particular time, if the person is an owner of a subject item of
  that type at the particular time and if the taxable amount of the
  subject item exceeds the price threshold in respect of the subject
  item, the person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada tax in
  respect of the subject item in the amount determined under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  particular time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a subject
  item that is owned by a person at the particular time at which the
  person ceases to be a registered vendor in respect of that type of
  subject item is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vehicle, the subject item
    was registered with the Government of Canada or a province before
    the particular time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the person is the only owner of the subject
      aircraft, the person is a qualifying aircraft user at the
      particular time, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, each person that is an owner
      of the subject aircraft is a qualifying aircraft user at the
      particular time;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft or subject vessel
    (other than a select subject vessel), the subject item is a
    qualifying subject aircraft or qualifying subject vessel of the
    person at the particular time; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft or subject
    vessel, a tax certificate in respect of the subject item is in
    effect at the particular time in accordance with section 37.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the taxable amount of a
  subject item owned by a person is, for the purposes of this section
  and for the purposes of determining under section 34 the amount of tax
  payable under this section, the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1363720 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the retail value of the subject item at the time at which the
      person ceases to be a registered vendor in respect of that type of
      subject item; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- ceasing to be qualifying aircraft
user

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, if a person
  ceases to be a qualifying aircraft user at a particular time, if the
  person is an owner of a subject aircraft at the particular time and if
  the taxable amount of the subject aircraft exceeds the price threshold
  in respect of the subject aircraft, the person must pay to Her Majesty
  in right of Canada tax in respect of the subject aircraft in the
  amount determined under section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) becomes payable at the
  particular time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (1) in respect of a subject
  aircraft that is owned by a person at the particular time at which the
  person ceases to be a qualifying aircraft user is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject aircraft is a qualifying subject
    aircraft of the person at the particular time; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a tax certificate in respect of the subject
    aircraft is in effect at the particular time in accordance with
    section 37.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the taxable amount of a
  subject aircraft owned by a person is, for the purposes of this
  section and for the purposes of determining under section 34 the
  amount of tax payable under this section, the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#1363737 .Subsection}
  A + B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the retail value of the subject aircraft at the time at which
      the person ceases to be a qualifying aircraft user; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxable amount --- regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and for the purposes
  of determining under section 34 the amount of tax payable under this
  section, the taxable amount of a subject item in prescribed
  circumstances is to be determined in prescribed manner.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Tax on Improvements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363743 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules --- improvement after sale

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 31, for the
  purposes of this section and for the purposes of determining under
  section 35 the amount of tax payable under this section, if a vendor
  sells a subject item to a particular purchaser, the following rules
  apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the improvement period in respect of the subject
    item is the period that begins on the day that an agreement for the
    sale is entered into and ends on

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the subject item is subsequently sold to a
      person that deals at arm's length with the particular purchaser
      and if the sale to the person is completed on a particular day
      that is before the day that is one year after the day on which the
      sale to the particular purchaser is completed, the particular day,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the day that is one year
      after the day on which the sale to the particular purchaser is
      completed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the unimproved taxable amount of the subject item
    is equal to the taxable amount of the subject item as determined
    under section 18 in respect of the sale to the particular purchaser;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net improvement amount of the subject item is
    equal to the total of all amounts, each of which is the value of the
    consideration for an improvement in respect of the subject item that
    is completed at any time during the improvement period in respect of
    the subject item but only to the extent that the value of the
    consideration is not included in the determination of the unimproved
    taxable amount of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- improvement after sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a person must pay to Her Majesty
  in right of Canada tax in respect of a subject item in the amount
  determined under section 35 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vendor sells the subject item to the person and
    tax in respect of the subject item becomes payable under section 18;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the net improvement amount of the subject item is
    greater than or equal to \$5,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (2) in respect of a subject
  item becomes payable at the beginning of the day following the day on
  which the improvement period in respect of the subject item ends.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules --- improvement in other
circumstances

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 31, for the
  purposes of this section and for the purposes of determining under
  section 35 the amount of tax payable under this section, if tax in
  respect of a subject item becomes payable by a person on a particular
  day under any of sections 20 and 23 to 28, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the improvement period in respect of the subject
    item is the period that begins on the particular day and ends on

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the subject item is subsequently sold to
      another person that deals at arm's length with the person and the
      sale is completed before the day that is one year after the
      particular day, the day on which the sale is completed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the day that is one year
      after the particular day;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the unimproved taxable amount of the subject item
    is equal to the taxable amount of the subject item as determined
    under whichever of those sections is applicable; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net improvement amount of the subject item is
    equal to the total of all amounts, each of which is the value of the
    consideration for an improvement in respect of the subject item that
    is completed at any time during the improvement period in respect of
    the subject item but only to the extent that the value of the
    consideration is not included in the determination of the unimproved
    taxable amount of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax --- improvement in other
  circumstances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a person must pay to Her Majesty
  in right of Canada tax in respect of a subject item in the amount
  determined under section 35 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tax in respect of the subject item becomes payable
    on a particular day by the person under any of sections 20 and 23 to
    28; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the net improvement amount of the subject item is
    greater than or equal to \$5,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When tax payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tax under subsection (2) in respect of a subject
  item becomes payable at the beginning of the day following the day on
  which the improvement period in respect of the subject item ends.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Improvement period --- regulations

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Subdivision, if prescribed circumstances exist,
  the improvement period in respect of a subject item is a prescribed
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unimproved taxable amount ---
  regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Subdivision and for the
  purposes of determining under section 35 the amount of tax payable
  under this Subdivision in respect of a subject item, if prescribed
  circumstances exist, the unimproved taxable amount of the subject item
  is to be determined in prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net improvement amount --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Subdivision and for the
  purposes of determining under section 35 the amount of tax payable
  under this Subdivision in respect of a subject item, if prescribed
  circumstances exist, the net improvement amount of the subject item is
  to be determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-arm's length --- joint and several,
or solidary, liability

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** If tax in respect of a
subject item is payable by a particular person under section 29 or 30
and if ownership of the subject item is transferred at any time during
the improvement period in respect of the subject item to another person
that is not dealing at arm's length with the particular person, the
other person is jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable with the
particular person for the payment of the tax.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[General Rules]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363781 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax not payable --- regulations

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** Tax under this Act in
respect of a subject item is not payable if prescribed circumstances
exist.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of tax --- general

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** The amount of tax payable
under this Division (other than Subdivision D) in respect of a subject
item is equal to the lesser of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1363787 .Paragraph}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the taxable amount of the subject item, and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is 10%, and
  :::

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1363792 .Paragraph}
  (C − D) × E

  where

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the taxable amount of the subject item,

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the price threshold in respect of the subject item, and

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is 20%.
  :::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of tax --- improvement

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** The amount of tax payable
under Subdivision D in respect of a subject item is equal to the amount
determined by the formula

::: {#1363799 .Section}
(A − B) + C

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the amount that would be the amount of tax payable in respect of
    the subject item if that amount of tax were determined under section
    34 and the taxable amount of the subject item were equal to the
    total of the unimproved taxable amount of the subject item and the
    net improvement amount of the subject item;

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the amount that would be the amount of tax payable in respect of
    the subject item if that amount of tax were determined under section
    34 and the taxable amount of the subject item were equal to the
    unimproved taxable amount of the subject item; and

[C]{.dfn}
:   is a prescribed amount.
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1363804 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, an exemption certificate applies in respect
  of the sale of a subject item by a vendor to a purchaser only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the certificate is made in prescribed form
    containing prescribed information;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the certificate includes

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identification number of the subject item,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one of the following declarations by the
      purchaser:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a declaration that the purchaser is a
        registered vendor in respect of that type of subject item at the
        time at which the sale is completed,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft, a
        declaration that the purchaser is a qualifying aircraft user at
        the time at which the sale is completed,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft, a
        declaration that the subject item is, at the time at which
        ownership of the subject item is transferred to the purchaser, a
        qualifying subject aircraft of the purchaser, or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vessel (other than a
        select subject vessel), a declaration that the subject item is,
        at the time at which ownership of the subject item is
        transferred to the purchaser, a qualifying subject vessel of the
        purchaser,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the certificate includes the declaration in
      clause (ii)(A), the registration number assigned to the purchaser
      under subsection 51(2), and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an acknowledgement by the purchaser that the
      purchaser is assuming liability to pay any amount of tax in
      respect of the subject item that is or may become payable by the
      purchaser under this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser provides, in a manner satisfactory to
    the Minister, the certificate in respect of the sale to the vendor;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the vendor retains the certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate --- multiple
  purchasers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, if a subject item is
  sold by a vendor to more than one purchaser, an exemption certificate
  applies in respect of the sale of the subject item only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the absence of this subsection, an exemption
    certificate would apply in respect of each purchaser in accordance
    with subsection (1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the declaration that is made by each purchaser
    under subparagraph (1)(b)(ii) is made under the same clause of that
    subparagraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), for the purposes of
  this Act, if prescribed circumstances exist, an exemption certificate
  applies in respect of a sale of a subject item.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax certificate

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person must send to the Minister an application for a tax certificate
  in respect of a subject item that is a subject aircraft or subject
  vessel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tax in respect of the subject item became payable
    by the person on a particular day;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is the case that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the tax became payable under section 20, the
      tax was paid in accordance with that section, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the person has filed with the
      Minister the return for the reporting period that includes the
      particular day and the person has taken into account the tax in
      determining the net tax for that reporting period;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where a rebate is or was available in
    respect of the subject item under Division 4, the amount of the
    rebate is less than the amount of the tax; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed conditions, if any, are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application under subsection (1) in respect of a
  subject item must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} include the identification number of the subject
    item;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify the date on which the tax in respect of the
    subject item became payable;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} include evidence satisfactory to the Minister that
    the conditions described in subparagraph (1)(b)(i) or (ii), as the
    case may be, are met; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} be made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application under subsection (1) in respect of a
  subject item must be filed with the Minister in prescribed manner on
  or before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the particular day that is one year after the day
    on which the tax in respect of the subject item became payable; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any day after the particular day that the Minister
    may allow.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of tax certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Upon receipt of an application under subsection (1)
  in respect of a subject item, the Minister must consider, with all due
  dispatch, the application and, if the Minister is satisfied that the
  conditions described in that subsection are met in respect of the
  subject item, issue a tax certificate in respect of the subject item
  specifying

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identification number of the subject item;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the tax certificate came into
    effect; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other information that is prescribed by
    regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tax certificate --- in effect

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a tax certificate in
  respect of a subject item that is issued under subsection (4) is
  deemed to be in effect beginning immediately after the time at which
  the tax in respect of the subject item became payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Existing certificate

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must not issue a tax certificate in
  respect of a subject item if another tax certificate has previously
  been issued in respect of the subject item under subsection (4) and
  the other tax certificate has not been revoked under subsection (10).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement to applicant

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} After considering an application under subsection (1)
  in respect of a subject item, the Minister must send, or make
  available in electronic format, to the applicant a statement
  specifying whether or not a tax certificate was issued under
  subsection (4) in respect of the subject item and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a tax certificate in respect of the subject item
    was issued because of the application, the Minister must send, or
    make available in electronic format, to the applicant with the
    statement a copy of, or the information included on, the tax
    certificate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister does not issue a tax certificate
    because there exists a previously issued tax certificate in respect
    of the subject item that has not been revoked under subsection (10),
    the Minister must send, or make available in electronic format, to
    the applicant with the statement a copy of, or the information
    included on, the previously issued tax certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request by third party

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} On request made by a person to the Minister in
  prescribed manner, the Minister must send, or make available in
  electronic format, to the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement specifying

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} whether a tax certificate in respect of a subject
      item has been issued under subsection (4), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if a tax certificate in respect of the subject
      item has been issued under subsection (4), whether a notice of
      revocation in respect of the tax certificate has been issued under
      subsection (10);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a tax certificate in respect of the subject item
    has been issued under subsection (4), a copy of, or the information
    included on, the tax certificate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a notice of revocation in respect of a tax
    certificate in respect of the subject item has been issued under
    subsection (10), a copy of, or the information included on, the
    notice of revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of change

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If a tax certificate in respect of a subject item was
  issued under subsection (4), if the tax certificate has not been
  revoked under subsection (10) and if the person that applied for the
  tax certificate under subsection (1) becomes aware at any time that
  the conditions described in subsection (1) are not met in respect of
  the subject item, the person must without delay provide notice in
  writing to the Minister that the conditions in subsection (1) are not
  met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If a tax certificate has been issued in respect of a
  subject item under subsection (4) and the tax certificate has not
  already been revoked under this subsection and the Minister becomes
  aware that the conditions described in subsection (1) are not met in
  respect of the subject item, the Minister must with all due dispatch

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} revoke the tax certificate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue a notice of revocation in respect of the tax
    certificate specifying

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identification number of the subject item,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the revocation, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any information that is prescribed by
      regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- timing

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a tax certificate
  issued under subsection (4) that has been revoked under
  subsection (10) is deemed never to have been in effect on any day on
  or after the effective day specified in the notice of revocation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for special import
certificate

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person that intends to import a subject item that is a subject
  aircraft or subject vessel (other than a select subject vessel) may
  apply to the Minister for a special import certificate in respect of
  the importation of the subject item if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time at which the subject item is accounted
    for in accordance with section 32 of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the person is an owner of the subject item
    and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is a qualifying subject aircraft
      or qualifying subject vessel of the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft, the subject
      item is accounted for in accordance with section 32 of the
      [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) solely by the person and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the person is the only owner of the subject
        aircraft, the person is a qualifying aircraft user, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, each person that is an owner
        of the subject aircraft is a qualifying aircraft user; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribed conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application made by a person in respect of a
  subject item under subsection (1) must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} include a declaration by the person specifying
    which of the conditions described in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) are met
    in respect of the subject item;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include the identification number of the subject
    item;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} include the person's name;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} be made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} be filed with the Minister in prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of special import certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Upon receipt of an application made by a person in
  respect of a subject item under subsection (1), the Minister must,
  with all due dispatch, consider the application and, if the Minister
  is satisfied that the conditions in respect of the subject item
  described in that subsection are met, issue and send to the person a
  special import certificate in respect of the importation of the
  subject item by the person specifying

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the identification number of the subject item;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the special import certificate is
    issued; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other information that is prescribed by
    regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special import certificate --- in
  effect

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a special import
  certificate that is issued under subsection (3) in respect of the
  importation of a subject item is deemed to be in effect beginning on
  the day on which the special import certificate is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of non-issuance

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, after considering an application made by a person
  in respect of a subject item under subsection (1), the Minister does
  not issue to the person a special import certificate in respect of the
  importation of the subject item by the person, the Minister must send
  a notice to the person specifying that a special import certificate
  was not issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of change

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a special import certificate in respect of the
  importation of a subject item was issued to a person under subsection
  (3), if the special import certificate has not been revoked under
  subsection (7) and if the person becomes aware that the conditions
  described in subsection (1) are not met in respect of the subject
  item, the person must without delay provide notice in writing to the
  Minister that the conditions in subsection (1) are not met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a special import certificate in respect of the
  importation of a subject item was issued to a person under subsection
  (3), if the special import certificate has not already been revoked
  under this subsection and if the Minister becomes aware that the
  conditions described in subsection (1) are not met in respect of the
  subject item, the Minister must with all due dispatch

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} revoke the special import certificate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue, and send to the person, a notice of
    revocation in respect of the special import certificate specifying

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identification number of the subject item,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the effective date of the revocation, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any information that is prescribed by
      regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- in effect

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a special import
  certificate issued under subsection (3) that has been revoked under
  subsection (7) is deemed to cease to have effect on the effective day
  specified in the notice of revocation.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Rebates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1363913 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Rebates to Net Tax]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363914 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate to net tax --- export

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  sale of a subject item to a purchaser by a vendor is completed at a
  particular time and the purchaser exports the subject item at a later
  time, the Minister must pay to the vendor a rebate in respect of the
  reporting period of the vendor that includes the later time if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following conditions are met:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the vendor is a registered vendor in respect of
      that type of subject item at the particular time,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser is not, at any time during the
      period beginning at the particular time and ending at the later
      time, a registered vendor in respect of that type of subject item,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} tax under section 18 in respect of the sale of
      the subject item becomes payable by the vendor at the particular
      time and the tax is taken into account in the determination of the
      net tax for the reporting period of the vendor that includes the
      particular time,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is not used in Canada at any
      time before the later time except to the extent reasonably
      necessary or incidental to its manufacture, offering for sale,
      transportation or exportation,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the subject item is not registered with the
      Government of Canada or a province before the later time except if
      the registration is done solely for a purpose incidental to its
      manufacture, offering for sale, transportation or exportation,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser exports the subject item as soon
      after the sale is completed as is reasonable having regard to the
      circumstances surrounding the exportation, the sale and, if
      applicable, the normal business practice of the purchaser and
      vendor, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser provides to the vendor, and the
      vendor retains, evidence satisfactory to the Minister of the
      exportation of the subject item by the purchaser; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribed conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a rebate under subsection (1) in
  respect of a sale of a subject item is equal to the amount of the tax
  in respect of the sale referred to in subparagraph (1)(a)(iii).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate to net tax --- regulations

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must pay a
rebate in respect of a subject item to a prescribed person in the amount
determined in prescribed manner if prescribed circumstances exist.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for rebate to net tax

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** Despite any other
provision of this Act, a rebate under this Subdivision in respect of a
particular reporting period of a person is not to be paid unless an
application for the rebate

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is made in prescribed form containing prescribed
  information; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is filed with the Minister in prescribed manner

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on or before the day on or before which the return
    under section 55 is required to be filed for the last reporting
    period of the person that ends within two years after the end of the
    particular reporting period, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the return in respect of the reporting period
    in which the amount of the rebate is taken into account in
    determining the net tax for the reporting period.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Other Rebates]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363937 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- foreign representative

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must pay to a person a rebate in respect of a sale of a
  subject item from a vendor to the person if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tax under section 18 is payable by the vendor in
    respect of the sale of the subject item and the tax is taken into
    account in the determination of the net tax for the reporting period
    of the vendor that includes the time at which the sale is completed;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is entitled to tax relief privileges
    under the [Foreign Missions and International Organizations
    Act](/eng/acts/F-29.4) for the tax payable under subsection 165(1)
    of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) in respect of the sale.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a rebate payable under subsection (1)
  in respect of a sale of a subject item is equal to the amount of the
  tax in respect of the sale referred to in paragraph (1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for rebate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Act, a rebate
  under this section is not to be paid unless an application for the
  rebate is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} filed with the Minister in prescribed manner within
    two years after the day on which the sale is completed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- payment in error

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must pay a rebate to a person if the person paid an amount in
  excess of the amount that was payable by the person under this Act
  whether the amount was paid by mistake or otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a rebate payable under subsection (1)
  by the Minister is the amount of the excess referred to in that
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on rebate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A rebate under this section in respect of an amount
  is not to be paid to a person to the extent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount was taken into account as an amount
    required to be paid by the person in respect of a reporting period
    of the person and the Minister has assessed the person for that
    period under section 92; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount was an amount assessed under section 92.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for rebate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Act, a rebate
  under this section is not to be paid unless an application for the
  rebate is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} filed with the Minister in prescribed manner within
    two years after the earlier of the day that the amount was taken
    into account in determining the net tax for a reporting period of
    the person and the day that the amount was paid to the Receiver
    General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One application per quarter

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Not more than one application for a rebate under this
  section may be made by a person in a calendar quarter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- regulations

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must pay a
rebate in respect of a subject item to a prescribed person in the amount
determined in prescribed manner if prescribed circumstances exist.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on rebate

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** A rebate under this
Subdivision is not to be paid to a person at any time unless all returns
of which the Minister has knowledge and that are required to be filed at
or before that time by the person under this Act, the [Excise Tax
Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air
Travellers Security Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Excise Act,
2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), Part 1 of the [Greenhouse Gas Pollution Pricing
Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55), the [Underused Housing Tax
Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Digital Services Tax Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65)
and the [Global Minimum Tax Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3) have been filed with
the Minister.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[General Rules for Rebates]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1363968 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on rebate

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** A rebate of an amount is
not to be paid to a person under this Division

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that it can reasonably be regarded that
  the person has obtained or is entitled to obtain a rebate, refund or
  remission of the amount under any other Division of this Act or under
  any other Act of Parliament; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single application

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** Only one application may
be made under this Division for a rebate with respect to any matter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- bankruptcy

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** If a trustee is appointed
under the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) to act in the
administration of the estate or succession of a bankrupt, a rebate under
this Division that the bankrupt was entitled to claim before the
appointment must not be paid after the appointment unless all returns
required to be filed in respect of the bankrupt under this Act, the
[Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),
the [Air Travellers Security Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Excise
Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), Part 1 of the [Greenhouse Gas Pollution
Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55), the [Underused Housing Tax
Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Digital Services Tax Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65)
and the [Global Minimum Tax Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3) in respect of periods
ending before the appointment have been filed and all amounts required
to be paid under that Part and those Acts by the bankrupt in respect of
those periods have been paid.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory recovery rights

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** Except as specifically
provided under this Act, the [Financial Administration
Act](/eng/acts/F-11) or the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), no person
has a right to recover any money paid to Her Majesty in right of Canada
as or on account of, or that has been taken into account by Her Majesty
in right of Canada as, an amount payable under this Act.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Registration, Reporting Periods, Returns and Requirement to Pay]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1363979 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying sale

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, a person makes a qualifying sale of a
  subject item if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person sells the subject item to a purchaser;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject item

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vehicle, has never been
      registered with the Government of Canada or a province other than
      a registration that is done only because of the sale, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft or subject
      vessel, has never been registered with the Government of Canada
      other than a registration that is done solely for a purpose
      incidental to its manufacture, offering for sale or
      transportation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the price threshold in respect of the subject item
    is less than the greatest of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the consideration for the sale,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
      particular time at which the sale is completed, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the subject item has a manufacturer's
      suggested retail price, the amount that would be the retail value
      of the subject item at the particular time if the fair market
      value of the subject item at the particular time were equal to the
      manufacturer's suggested retail price of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying importation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a person makes a
  qualifying importation of a subject item if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person imports the subject item;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject item

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject vehicle, has never been
      registered with the Government of Canada or a province other than
      a registration that is done only because of the importation, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a subject aircraft or subject
      vessel, has never been registered with the Government of Canada
      other than a registration that is done solely for a purpose
      incidental to its manufacture, offering for sale or
      transportation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the price threshold in respect of the subject item
    is exceeded by the greatest of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the taxable amount of the subject item determined
      under section 20 in respect of the importation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the retail value of the subject item at the
      particular time at which the subject item is accounted for in
      accordance with section 32 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)
      in respect of the importation, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the subject item has a manufacturer's
      suggested retail price, the amount that would be the retail value
      of the subject item at the particular time if the fair market
      value of the subject item at the particular time were equal to the
      manufacturer's suggested retail price of the subject item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person is required to
  be registered as a vendor in respect of a type of subject item if the
  person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} makes a qualifying sale, or a qualifying
    importation, of a subject item of that type in the course of a
    business of offering for sale in Canada subject items of that type
    that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subject vehicles, have never been
      registered with the Government of Canada or a province, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subject aircraft and subject
      vessels, have never been registered with the Government of Canada
      other than registrations that are done solely for a purpose
      incidental to the manufacture, offering for sale or transportation
      of the subject items; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person that is required under subsection (3) to be
  registered as a vendor in respect of a type of subject item must apply
  to the Minister for registration on or before the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the first qualifying sale of a
    subject item of that type made by the person is completed,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which, for the first qualifying
    importation of a subject item of that type made by the person, the
    subject item is accounted for in accordance with section 32 of the
    [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed conditions are met, the prescribed
    day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration permitted

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a person that is not
  required under this section to be registered as a vendor in respect of
  a type of subject item may apply to the Minister to be registered as a
  vendor in respect of that type of subject item if the person is a
  prescribed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration not required

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), a person is not required to
  be registered as a vendor in respect of a type of subject item for the
  purposes of this Act if the person is a prescribed person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for registration

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for registration under this Division is to be made in
  prescribed form containing prescribed information and is to be filed
  with the Minister in prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of registration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person that meets the requirements for
  registration under this Division applies for registration, the
  Minister may register the person and, upon doing so, the Minister must
  assign a registration number to the person for the purposes of this
  Act and notify the person of the registration number and of the
  effective date of the registration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of intent --- failure to apply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister has reason to believe that a person
  is not registered as a vendor in respect of a type of subject item for
  the purposes of this Act, is required to be so registered under this
  Division and has failed to apply for registration under this Division
  as and when required, the Minister may send a notice in writing (in
  this section referred to as a "notice of intent") to the person that
  the Minister proposes to so register the person under subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations to Minister

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Upon receipt of a notice of intent, a person must
  apply for the registration under this Division proposed in the notice
  or establish to the satisfaction of the Minister that the person is
  not required to be so registered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration by Minister

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, after 60 days after the particular day on which a
  notice of intent was sent by the Minister to a person, the person has
  not applied under this Division for the registration in respect of a
  type of subject item proposed in the notice and the Minister is not
  satisfied that the person is not required to be so registered, the
  Minister may register the person as a vendor in respect of that type
  of subject item under this Division and, upon doing so, must assign a
  registration number to the person for the purposes of this Act and
  notify the person of the registration number and of the effective date
  of the registration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of registration

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, after giving a person that is registered under this
  Division reasonable written notice, cancel a registration of the
  person as a vendor in respect of a type of subject item under this
  Division if the Minister is satisfied that the registration is not
  required for the purposes of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person files with the Minister in prescribed
  manner a request, in prescribed form containing prescribed
  information, to have a registration of the person as a vendor in
  respect of a type of subject item cancelled, the Minister must cancel
  the registration of the person if the Minister is satisfied that the
  registration is not required for the purposes of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation in prescribed
  circumstances

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must cancel a registration of a person
  as a vendor in respect of a type of subject item under this Division
  in prescribed circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of cancellation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister cancels a registration of a person
  under this Division, the Minister must notify the person of the
  cancellation and the effective date of the cancellation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security --- registration

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, the Minister may require a person that
  applies to be registered, or that is required to be registered, under
  this Division to give and maintain security, in an amount determined
  by the Minister and subject to any terms and conditions that the
  Minister may specify, for the payment of any amount that is or may
  become payable by the person under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security --- importation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the Minister may
  require a person referred to in subsection 20(1) to give and maintain
  security, in an amount determined by the Minister and subject to any
  terms and conditions that the Minister may specify, for the payment of
  any amount that is or may become payable by the person under this Act,
  if the provisions of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or any other laws relating to customs
  under which security may be required do not apply to the payment of
  that amount.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to comply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, a person referred to in
  subsection (1) or (2) fails to give or maintain security in an amount
  satisfactory to the Minister, the Minister may retain as security, out
  of any amount that may be or may become payable to the person under
  this Act, the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), Part 1 of the [Greenhouse Gas Pollution
  Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55), the [Underused Housing Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Digital Services Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65) or the [Global Minimum Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3), an amount not exceeding the amount determined
  by the formula

  ::: {#1364043 .Subsection}
  A -- B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount of security that would, at that time, be
      satisfactory to the Minister if it were given by the person in
      accordance with subsection (1) or (2), as the case may be; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount of security, if any, given and maintained by the
      person in accordance with subsection (1) or (2), as the case may
      be.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount deemed paid

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount retained under subsection (3) is deemed to
  have been paid, at the time referred to in that subsection, by the
  Minister to the person, and to have been given, immediately after that
  time, by the person as security in accordance with subsection (1) or
  (2), as the case may be.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting periods

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, a reporting period of a person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before 2023, the period that begins on September 1,
    2022 and ends on December 31, 2022; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after 2022

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} unless subparagraph (ii) applies, a calendar
      quarter, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed conditions are met, a prescribed
      period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting period --- registration or
  cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if at a particular time the
  Minister registers a person, or cancels a registration of a person,
  under this Division

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the particular reporting period of the person that
    includes the particular time ends on the day that includes the
    particular time; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reporting period of the person begins on the day
    after the day that includes the particular time and ends on the day
    that is the last day of the calendar quarter that includes the
    particular time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of return

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person that is registered or required to be registered under this
  Division must file a return with the Minister for each reporting
  period of the person. The return is to be filed on or before the last
  day of the first month after the end of the reporting period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of return --- non-registered
  persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person that is not registered and not required
  to be registered under this Division must file a return with the
  Minister for each reporting period of the person in which tax (other
  than tax under section 20) becomes payable by the person. The return
  is to be filed on or before the last day of the first month after the
  end of the reporting period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of return --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), if prescribed
  circumstances exist, a return for a reporting period that is a
  prescribed reporting period is to be filed with the Minister in
  accordance with prescribed rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing not required --- regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), if prescribed
  circumstances exist, a return for a reporting period that is a
  prescribed reporting period is not required to be filed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and content

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** Every return that is
required to be filed under section 55 is to be made in prescribed form
containing prescribed information and is to be filed in prescribed
manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net tax --- obligation

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person that is required to file a return under section 55 must, in the
  return, determine the net tax for the reporting period of the person
  for which the return is required to be filed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of net tax

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, the net tax for a particular
  reporting period of a person is, for the purposes of this Act, the
  positive or negative amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#1364076 .Subsection}
  (A − B) + C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount of tax
      (other than tax under section 20) that becomes payable by the
      person in the particular reporting period;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount of a
      rebate under Subdivision A of Division 4 payable by the Minister
      in respect of a reporting period and that is claimed by the person
      in the return under section 55 for the particular reporting
      period; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is a prescribed amount.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to pay

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the net tax for a reporting period of a person is
  a positive amount, the person must pay that amount to the Receiver
  General on or before the day on or before which the return for the
  reporting period is required to be filed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate of net tax

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the net tax for a reporting period of a person is
  a negative amount, the person may claim in the return for the
  reporting period the amount of that net tax as a rebate for the
  reporting period payable to the person by the Minister. The Minister
  must pay the rebate to the person with all due dispatch after the
  return is filed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- rebate of net tax

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister is not required to pay a rebate under
  subsection (4) to a person unless the Minister is satisfied that all
  information, that is contact information or that is information
  relating to the identification and business activities of the person,
  to be given by the person on any application for registration made by
  the person under this Division has been provided and is accurate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- rebate of net tax

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A rebate under subsection (4) is not to be paid to a
  person at any time unless all returns of which the Minister has
  knowledge and that are required to be filed at or before that time by
  the person under this Act, the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air Travellers Security Charge
  Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), Part
  1 of the [Greenhouse Gas Pollution Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55),
  the [Underused Housing Tax Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5), the [Digital
  Services Tax Act](/eng/acts/D-1.65) and the [Global Minimum Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/G-3.3) have been filed with the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on rebate of net tax

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a rebate for a reporting period of a person is
  paid to the person under subsection (4), interest at the specified
  rate is to be paid to the person on the rebate for the period that
  begins on the day that is 30 days after the later of the day the
  return in which the rebate is claimed is filed with the Minister and
  the day following the last day of the reporting period and that ends
  on the day the rebate is paid.
